# V0.4.1.8 Atomic Ownership Authority Correction

## 1. Status, scope, and non-authorization

This is a **docs-only planning candidate** formed from `c6e0992f83846716e9f71b745c329e222af3468b`. It changes no format value, code, test, workflow, schema, asset, runtime, threshold, or existing plan body. Its only normative change is a narrow correction to ownership and route semantics: a source decision is disposed by non-overlapping atomic obligations, and each obligation has exactly one primary responsible owner and one responsibility kind.

Formation, review, merge, or later deletion of this document does **not** authorize R1 or its three assets, an R2 restart, C2, P3b-B/P3b-O, a public CLI, Ready, release, a functional claim, or merge. V0.4.1.3 C2 STOP, V0.4.1.4–.6 serial ordering/default-closed/non-delivery boundaries, and V0.4.1.7's three-asset R1 route and separate R2-restart authorization remain unchanged.

The rejected local candidate and abandoned diagnostic chains are neither inputs nor evidence. No mapping, owner, class, rationale, span, route, or digest below is derived from them.

Narrow precedence: this document supersedes earlier wording only where an ownership record, compound `P3`/`P5` route, Z-009 allocation, or route-intersection meaning conflicts with the atomic model below. It does not supersede values, thresholds, whitelist paths, artifact versions, lifecycle states, proof strength, serial order, default-off behavior, non-delivery status, C2 STOP, P3b ordering, or V0.4.1.7 R1/R2 authorization gates.

## 3. Atomic ownership model

An atomic obligation is the smallest independently checkable operation. `operation_key` is globally unique; no generic parent record exists. Registry representation, rule/migration asset production, DOCX execution, preservation, orchestration, verification, governance, and future boundary are separate operations even when they cite one source row. A source row may therefore have more than one obligation, but no operation appears twice and no obligation has more than one primary owner.

`candidate_route_stages` is the revised stage set contributed by the source mapping and later succession clauses. `route_intersection` is calculated exactly as `candidate_route_stages ∩ owner_catalog[owner].permitted_stages`, preserving candidate order. `selected_stage` is a distinct field and is populated only when that intersection has exactly one member. `target_stage` records a future destination without placing it in the current execution route. Governance and future-boundary records have an empty route intersection.

P3 is decomposed by operation: P3a-R owns registry 2.3/matrix 1.2 representation; P3b-B owns the formal-chain/first-batch A-F/H-J assets; P3b-O owns remaining complete-base assets. P5 is decomposed by object: P5a owns M/N execution, P5b owns the other complex-object execution. P6 owns internal orchestration, state, approvals, reports, and allowlist routing, but has **no field or layout write authority**. P7 owns only frozen target-Word finalization actions plus independent convergence/exit verification: it may repaginate under an approved configuration, update approved field results/formal TOC, and execute the frozen convergence sequence, but may not invent base rules; change field instructions, order, or boundaries; change section boundaries, starting values, or number formats; rebuild complex headers/footers; or govern a public interface.

Owner counts: P1=53, P3a-R=148, P3b-B=54, P3b-O=94, P4=52, P5a=23, P5b=68, P6=28, P7=37, PROJECT-GOVERNANCE=1, USER-GOVERNANCE=1, V042-PLAN=20, V043-PLAN=1. Responsibility counts: future_boundary=21, governance=2, implementation=479, preservation=57, verification=21. These are derived summaries from the sole appendix, not an independent ownership source.

All 21 current decisions whose candidate route contains P7 were audited. Source-backed P7 implementation appears only in: V040-C-007, V040-D-006, V040-D-008, V040-V-001, V040-V-006, V040-Z-001, V040-Z-002, V040-Z-004, V040-Z-011, V040-Z-012. Verification-only P7 decisions are: V040-C-005, V040-C-006, V040-D-001, V040-D-002, V040-D-003, V040-D-004, V040-D-005, V040-D-007, V040-U-005, V040-Z-007, V040-Z-009. Every P7-routed decision retains a separate verification obligation; an implementation obligation never self-certifies.

The three specifically corrected decisions have these non-overlapping runtime atoms:

- D-006: P1 preserves complex header/footer structure; P6 only orchestrates the approval/report path; P7 selectively updates approved PAGE-like field results and separately verifies the Word result.
- D-008: P6 establishes and approves the frozen pagination-boundary configuration; P1 preserves boundary/start/number-format invariants; P7 performs only approved-configuration Word repagination and whitelist field-result update, then independently verifies final acceptance.
- T-005: P5b formats reference form/position/superscript and separately performs only an approved reference-field result update; P1 preserves field structure; P6 only approves/orchestrates.

## 4. Z-009 correction

Z-009 is not assigned by matching the string `CLI`, and no whole-row owner exists. It is split into four non-overlapping obligations, each anchored in the frozen V0.4.0 semantic clause; its V0.4.1 route cell constrains routing only and creates no business-semantic obligation:

1. P6 implements and verifies that the callable surface remains an internal execution/test interface; this grants no public API status.
2. PROJECT-GOVERNANCE owns the current prohibition and separate-plan requirement for any public wrapper; it has no execution route.
3. P7 owns the full-project-test prerequisite as verification, not as CLI implementation.
4. USER-GOVERNANCE owns only the future explicit confirmation on a separately reviewed plan; that decision is not a current implementation stage.

## 5. Canonical appendix, digest, and fail-closed validation

The single fence labelled `ownership-machine-json` is the only machine ownership authority in this document. Prose and counts are explanations derived from it. Exact source rows and lifecycle mapping rows are bound by path, full blob OID, Unicode code-point half-open interval, literal, and literal SHA-256. Validation must read Git blobs from `c6e0992f83846716e9f71b745c329e222af3468b`, compare all eight frozen input identities plus the V0.4.1.7 precedence identity, reconstruct every literal from its interval, and fail on CR/LF normalization, duplicate/missing/extra decisions, or any additional ownership fence.

Raw appendix SHA-256: `3d0e63ded3f64eecc279056d1a6f2d193cc452c38cc6143d1c7cc9c22f309cfc`

Canonical appendix SHA-256: `26c04207fce751b600609e550b7369ed46b48bf99e90f157b3dcf975802653e6`

Raw digest scope and canonical serialization are defined inside the appendix. Neither digest is a signature or application authentication; coordinated modification of all local files remains outside this unsigned trust boundary.

Acceptance requires: exactly 171 semantic rows and 171/171 disposed decisions; no duplicate/missing/extra source decision; no generic/atomic overlap; no duplicate implementation operation; exact route intersections; valid owner/stage/permission references; Z-009's four source-backed assertions; no route-only literal used as an atomic semantic source; no external/future/governance record in an execution route; non-empty source-specific rationale with named exclusions; one JSON fence; raw/canonical round trip and both digests; INDEX link/order/status/precedence; an exact two-path diff; and `git diff --check`.

## 6. Rollback, human review, and non-claims

Rollback deletes this file and its INDEX row/precedence sentence only. It does not revive rejected candidates or diagnostic chains. Human review remains necessary for whether each atomic split fully preserves the source semantics; automated closure proves identity, uniqueness, references, and routing invariants, not the correctness of a semantic judgment.

This candidate does not claim R1 formed, R1 accepted, R2 authorized, registry or base implemented, C2 reopened, P3b started, Word validation complete, public CLI approved, Ready, release, or merge authority.

        "literal": "原稿已有正确、完整的前置页码边界时默认保持。需要重建时，结构确认阶段必须明确“第一个编号前置页”；序、前言、致谢等经确认需要编号的前置内容从阿拉伯数字 1 开始，目录与此前已编号前置内容连续；目录前没有其他编号前置内容时，目录首页为阿拉伯数字 1。",

    {
      "decision_id": "V040-A-001",

        "literal": "| V040-A-001 | MB-A-001 | P3→P4 | T41-A-001 | MIG-A-001：修改 FMT-PAGE-301，仅保留纸张保持 | 保持；节属性逐项审计 | - |",

      ],
      "primary_version": "V0.4.1",
      "semantic_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 3684,
        "codepoint_start": 3512,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-A-001 | 每节纸张大小、方向、页边距、装订线、页眉页脚距离和分栏默认保持原稿。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社 | 保持；修改前后节属性逐项审计。 | V0.4.1；多节、异向、异边距合成测试。 | FMT-PAGE-301 只保留 page-size preserve；比例边距与固定距离不再默认。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "7232feb9e07acc7cdde27866e15a9525ff7684d58f5c9130a9a50ee97dafdfc9",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"

    {
      "decision_id": "V040-A-002",
      "lifecycle_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 19651,
        "codepoint_start": 19564,
        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-A-002 | MB-A-002 | P3→P4 | T41-A-002 | MIG-A-002：废弃分栏隐式推断 | 保持/自动；仅批准新增节继承 | - |",
        "literal_sha256": "0aaf7b8cb6914d8598bd79b1f9590ba116e5385f122b1413d2ac3520a6241797",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"
      },
      "obligation_ids": [
        "V040-A-002:registry",
        "V040-A-002:asset",
        "V040-A-002:execution-p4"
      ],
      "primary_version": "V0.4.1",
      "semantic_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 3848,
        "codepoint_start": 3685,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-A-002 | 不自动把正文改为单栏或双栏；新增普通分节继承当前位置有效分栏；批准横向节结束后恢复此前分栏。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社 | 保持/自动；只有新增已批准分节时复制有效栏属性。 | V0.4.1；单栏、双栏、混合栏和横向往返测试。 | 撤销旧规则对分栏缺省值的隐式推断。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "73ce82aa4a8bf9fbf06e47739e49bb6affa985edf3da0696b9353096dbbc6ee9",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    },

        "literal": "| V040-A-003 | MB-A-003 | P3→P4 | T41-A-003 | MIG-A-003：废弃 FMT-HEAD-301 强制分页 | 保持；不得批量改分页 | - |",
        "literal_sha256": "6e8dee906d22ab0736831a19845091f1a748ec46cd21cf65a29c4b96c8cabc5b",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"

        "V040-A-003:registry",
        "V040-A-003:asset",
        "V040-A-003:execution-p4"

        "literal": "| V040-A-003 | 章题不强制新页，也不强制取消原有分页。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社 | 保持；不得批量设置或清除 page-break-before。 | V0.4.1；原有分页保留和无分页章题测试。 | FMT-HEAD-301 的强制段前分页废弃。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "b86d5ebbe3582b0ac5bf0b3f60237695e4f6dfb5eea70133de70dacc2a431aa3",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"

        "literal": "| V040-A-004 | MB-A-004 | P3→P4 | T41-A-004 | MIG-A-004：收紧 FMT-SECTION-301 为逐对象批准 | QA；位置/页码/页眉复验 | - |",
        "literal_sha256": "7534025d04f2b8fd8620646114b07d890a633c48694be3c87f1f5814825d0404",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"

      "semantic_source": {

        "codepoint_start": 3997,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-A-004 | 宽表或宽图只有经明确批准才建立前后横向分节；页码连续，页眉页脚保持正常阅读方向。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社；批准的工程模块只能提出候选 | QA；对象位置、页码和页眉页脚方向必须复验。 | V0.4.1；宽图/宽表批准与拒绝、连续页码测试。 | FMT-SECTION-301 改为逐对象批准，不再自动应用。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-A-005 | SAFE-A-005+MB-A-005 | P1/P3→P4 | T41-A-005 | MIG-A-005：收紧空白尾页清理 | 报告/保持/QA；仅清理可证明自产项 | - |",

        "literal": "| V040-A-005 | 空白页按原因分类，不执行“删除页面”；仅可受控清理由 Skill 产生的顶部空段或冗余尾节；版心奇偶页、含对象页、人工分页和表格导致的空白页保持或 QA。 | 安全层 + monograph-base | 不可覆盖删除边界；用户可决定保持/QA | 报告/保持/QA；清理项须有生成来源和允许变化记录。 | V0.4.1；五类空白页与幂等清理测试。 | 收紧旧空白尾页和冗余分节清理规则。 |",

        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"

        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-B-005 | 纯书名文字块可垂直居中；复杂书名页保持相对布局。书名页不显示页码。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社 | 自动/保持/QA；不得移动复杂对象或改变相对顺序。 | V0.4.1；纯文字垂直居中、复杂对象保持、页码隐藏测试。 | 吸收 V0.3.1 书名块定位，但禁止推广到复杂书名页。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "6f303bf6d6b14de0c6c1fca25eaec8ec1f85dacddebed3faf3d7d0c70b662872",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }

    {

        "literal": "| V040-B-006 | MB-B-006 | P3→P4 | T41-B-006 | MIG-B-006：拆除前置笼统推断 | 保持/QA；顺序和内容不改 | - |",

        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-B-006 | 版权页、题献、题词保持原稿并进入视觉 QA；前置内容顺序保持；前置标题默认不编号，是否进入目录由语义目录决定。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社可覆盖编号/目录选择 | 保持/QA；内容和对象顺序不可改。 | V0.4.1；多种前置角色、顺序、编号和 TOC 选择测试。 | 拆除旧 Profile 对前置部分的笼统样式推断。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "89fd75b4984d0da3b24e812bca2885c4bb63f64d5c98e16f3e9e0985827c76f7",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    },

        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-B-007 | MB-B-007 | P3→P4/P6 | T41-B-007 | MIG-B-007：补齐不编号区逻辑边界 | 保持/自动/QA；边界不明阻塞 | - |",
        "literal_sha256": "1a3bd0f8051fb5f8a09407d8a35cdc4e33667fb1f9ad0c4a676bfadb04499b0d",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"

        "codepoint_start": 5492,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-B-007 | 书名页、版权页、题献页和题词页组成前置不编号区：不显示页码，也不消耗前置逻辑页码。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社可明确覆盖具体边界 | 保持/自动/QA；角色或边界无法确认时 blocked_qa，不得按物理页序自动推断。 | V0.4.1；四类不编号页单独及组合测试，并验证后续首个编号页仍为逻辑页 1。 | 补齐旧规则只隐藏书名页页码、未定义是否消耗逻辑页码的边界。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "5627af169adafab64e24b61da42fd637e20b9a5c84f6cc9770e52c2128a87bd6",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    },

        "literal": "| V040-C-001 | MB-C-001 | P3→P4 | T41-C-001 | MIG-C-001：废弃字间空格做法 | 自动/报告；非标准题名须批准 | - |",

        "literal": "| V040-C-001 | 题名显示文字为“目录”，不得用字间空格制造视觉间距。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社可覆盖显示文本；空格模拟仍禁止 | 自动/报告；不得改写原有非标准题名而不经批准。 | V0.4.1；正常题名、空格题名和自定义题名测试。 | 取代历史“四个空格”视觉做法。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-C-003 | 默认收录章、二级、三级标题；四级仅在语义目录明确批准后收录。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社；模块不得扩大全局范围 | 自动/QA；来源必须与批准语义标题一一对应。 | V0.4.1；三级默认、四级批准/拒绝、越界来源测试。 | 收紧旧宽泛 heading-levels=4。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-C-005 | 章项宋体/Times New Roman 10.5 pt、加粗、段前 6 pt、段后 0；二级项 10.5 pt、不加粗、段前后 0；三级/四级项 9 pt、不加粗、段前后 0。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社 | 自动；不得因字段更新丢失样式。 | V0.4.1；各级样式及 Word 刷新保持测试。 | FMT-TOC-301 的字号/字重拆分并补齐四级与段距。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-C-006 | 使用点引导线、右对齐页码和真实 Word 动态 TOC；来源仅限批准语义标题；标题对象污染时整份切换为纯文本 TC 来源。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社可覆盖外观；动态字段和纯净来源不可覆盖 | 自动/QA；字段可编辑，TC 文本与标题逐项一致。 | V0.4.1；Heading/TC 两模式和字段可编辑测试。 | 延续 V0.3.3，替代静态目录和混合来源。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-C-007 | TOC 结果不得含图片、DrawingML/VML、OLE、文本框、表格、空项、重复项或未批准来源。每章只更新目录源清单和状态；全书结束统一生成正式 TOC。 | 安全层 + monograph-base | 不可覆盖 | 阻塞/报告；污染结果拒绝回写。 | V0.4.1；各污染类型、重复/空项、章节阶段不生成 TOC 测试。 | 延续 V0.3.3 并纳入长文阶段门禁。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "a220e7423e2a43284957e26306b5f09cb2cf5cbd389ef440545f0b72a0270d07",

        "literal": "| V040-D-001 | 经批准进入编号系统的前置部分和目录使用阿拉伯数字；第一个编号前置页从 1 开始，目录首页显示其连续逻辑页码。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社 | 自动；分节和编号边界必须获批准。 | V0.4.1；一页/多页编号前置内容、无编号区和目录连续起始测试。 | FMT-TOC-301 的 lower_roman 废弃，并与新增不编号区边界协调。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-D-002 | 正文独立使用阿拉伯数字，从 1 开始，后续章节连续。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社 | 自动；不得逐章重启。 | V0.4.1；正文重启一次、章节/横向节连续测试。 | 延续 V0.2.5 双分区并移入基础层。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "e1aed01cb4eccde8a5ce8bae2eb35b092b9dc05aad791cd00bf8ae495482c2b1",

        "literal": "| V040-D-003 | 页码位于页脚外侧：奇数页右、偶数页左；页码宋体/Times New Roman 9 pt，裸阿拉伯数字。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社 | 自动；必须同时存在奇偶页脚关系。 | V0.4.1；奇偶页位置、字体和裸数字测试。 | FMT-HEADER-301 的镜像外侧位置保留并补齐精确字体。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-D-004 | 章首页隐藏运行页眉但显示页码；有意空白页参与计数但不显示运行页眉和页码。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社 | 自动/QA；必须区分章首页和有意空白页。 | V0.4.1；章首页、有意空白页和连续计数测试。 | FMT-HEADER-301 的章首页隐藏页码改为显示。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-D-006 | SAFE-D-006+MB-D-006 | P1/P3→P6/P7 | T41-D-006 | MIG-D-006：保留选择性回写，废弃 update-all | 保持/QA；未知字段不改 | V0.4.4 Word 复验 |",

        "literal": "| V040-D-006 | 复杂页眉页脚默认保留，不自动重建；PAGE 等字段只做选择性更新。 | 安全层 + monograph-base | 用户/出版社可批准局部格式；选择性更新不可覆盖 | 保持/QA；未知关系和未批准字段不改。 | V0.4.1；复杂关系、首页/奇偶组合和未知字段测试。 | 延续 V0.3.2 选择性回写，废弃全量 update-all。 |",

    {
      "decision_id": "V040-D-007",
      "lifecycle_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 22062,
        "codepoint_start": 21955,
        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-D-007 | MB-D-007 | P3→P4/P6/P7 | T41-D-007 | MIG-D-007：细化前置首个编号页 | 保持/自动/QA；不自动推断 | V0.4.4 Word 复验 |",
        "literal_sha256": "24746659ffb72159496216baa02e0d97cd50e86be4a17c6d098eea3a1ab28a13",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"
      },
      "obligation_ids": [
        "V040-D-007:registry",
        "V040-D-007:asset",
        "V040-D-007:execution-p4",
        "V040-D-007:orchestration",
        "V040-D-007:word-verification"
      ],
      "primary_version": "V0.4.1",
      "semantic_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 8513,
        "codepoint_start": 8198,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-D-007 | 原稿已有正确、完整的前置页码边界时默认保持。需要重建时，结构确认阶段必须明确“第一个编号前置页”；序、前言、致谢等经确认需要编号的前置内容从阿拉伯数字 1 开始，目录与此前已编号前置内容连续；目录前没有其他编号前置内容时，目录首页为阿拉伯数字 1。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社可覆盖具体边界和纳入编号的前置角色 | 保持/自动/QA；边界无法确认时 blocked_qa，不得自动推断。 | V0.4.1；保留有效原稿边界、前言从 1、目录从 1、目录连续承接前言测试。 | 细化 FMT-TOC-301 前置页码迁移，替代“所有前置页一律从 1”可能误含不编号区的解释。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "55ffae235608cb59c5e690ff62e7f4fa9ebeca76bf5b9ad8850bda342da43899",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    },
    {
      "decision_id": "V040-D-008",
      "lifecycle_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 22181,
        "codepoint_start": 22063,
        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-D-008 | SAFE-D-008+STATE-D-008 | P1/P6/P7 | T41-D-008 | MIG-D-008：拆分页码配置与最终化职责 | 自动/阻塞；批准边界绑定指纹 | V0.4.4 最终验收 |",
        "literal_sha256": "3834eecf8f36665bdf2aa80fdf95307ef0058e2fac008930790a1719334dcddb",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"
      },
      "obligation_ids": [
        "V040-D-008:pagination-boundary-preservation",
        "V040-D-008:configuration-approval-orchestration",
        "V040-D-008:approved-final-word-repagination-and-field-update",

      "semantic_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 8777,
        "codepoint_start": 8514,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-D-008 | 第 3 步页面/分节确认阶段建立并批准不编号区、第一个编号前置页和正文重启边界；最终 Word 分页/目录收敛阶段只能按既定配置重新分页和更新白名单字段，不得改变边界、起始值或编号格式。 | 安全层 + monograph-base | 用户/出版社只能在结构批准工件中改变；最终化阶段不可覆盖 | 自动/阻塞；批准边界绑定输入和最终 Profile 指纹。 | V0.4.1 状态契约、V0.4.4 Word 验收；边界锁定和收敛不改分节测试。 | 将既有分页配置与字段最终化职责明确分离。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "d5bc6f5400c6a00c5662222acfd4c6f7c68279acd86e497ef773281a693a4f7f",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    },

        "literal": "| V040-E-005 | MB-E-005 | P3→P4 | T41-E-005 | MIG-E-005：保留 V0.3.1 零缩进并补列表清理 | 自动；幂等且列表属性归零 | - |",

      "semantic_source": {

        "codepoint_start": 9598,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-E-005 | 所有标题首行、左、右、悬挂缩进均为 0；keep-with-next 和 keep-together；不得插入空白段落。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社可覆盖 keep 属性；零缩进和禁空段为安全格式默认 | 自动；列表属性必须同时清理。 | V0.4.1；字符/磅值/列表缩进及幂等测试。 | 延续 V0.3.1 标题零缩进并补 keep-together。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "10627b809a4b76f696bf69f2c53b192f9dbd33a1210f0219c30efa61904bbea9",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    },

        "literal": "| V040-E-007 | 根据最终语义目录重建 Word 关联多级列表；只删除批准旧编号前缀，不改标题文字。无上层编号方案时后备为“第1章、1.1、1.1.1、1.1.1.1”。附录按实际语义标题级别应用样式，不固定章题。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社可覆盖编号方案；安全内容边界不可覆盖 | 自动/QA；歧义前缀 blocked_qa，变化进入允许清单。 | V0.4.1；四级编号、重启、前缀批准/歧义和附录层级测试。 | FMT-FIELD-301 拆分为语义批准、前缀删除和编号重建。 |",

        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-F-006 | 标题、题注、公式、列表、引文、注释、表格、代码、提示框和图片段落不得误套正文规则；字符格式、超链接、书签、修订、批注、内容控件、OMML 和 OLE 保持。 | 安全层 + monograph-base | 不可覆盖保护边界 | 阻塞/保持；对象分类失败不得套正文。 | V0.4.1；各排除角色和受保护对象哈希测试。 | 收紧旧全局正文样式应用。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-G-001 | V0.4.2 science-engineering | SEM-G-001 | T42-G-001 | 仅保护字符运行，不应用科学字形 |",

        "literal": "| V040-G-001 | 变量、变量下标、任意函数、参数、几何点/线段/弧和标量物理量使用斜体。 | science-engineering-monograph | 用户/出版社；领域模块仅限自身对象 | 自动/QA；必须有语义证据，不能仅按字符形态判断。 | V0.4.2；正文、公式和上下标语义样例测试。 | 从私有科学符号摘要迁入类型增量，不进入 base。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-G-002 | 数字、已定义函数/算子、常量 e/i/π、微分 d、变分算子、单位、说明性下标、化学符号和型号代码使用正体。 | science-engineering-monograph | 用户/出版社；领域模块仅限自身对象 | 自动/QA；上下文歧义 blocked_qa。 | V0.4.2；常量/变量歧义、单位、化学式和型号测试。 | 新建领域语义规则，禁止全局字母斜体。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-G-003 | 向量采用粗斜体变量；矩阵和张量必须按语义确认，不可只用字符模式猜测。 | science-engineering-monograph | 用户/出版社；equation-heavy 可在公式对象内覆盖 | 自动/QA；矩阵/张量未确认即 blocked_qa。 | V0.4.2；向量、矩阵、张量同字符异义测试。 | 取代基于正则或周围加粗的推断。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-G-004 | V0.4.2 science-engineering | SEM-G-004 | T42-G-004 | 不建立私有符号表 |",

        "literal": "| V040-G-004 | 每个任务建立符号表；歧义对象进入 blocked_qa，禁止仅靠全局正则替换。 | science-engineering-monograph | 不可覆盖禁止正则替换；用户确认具体语义 | QA/报告；符号表不保存私有原文。 | V0.4.2；符号覆盖率、冲突和泄露测试。 | 私有符号规范只形成摘要和任务映射。 |",

    {

        "literal": "| V040-G-005 | 既有公式对象和数学字体保持；科学符号字形优先于关键词加粗等周围段落样式。 | 安全层 + science-engineering-monograph | 不可覆盖对象保护；用户可确认符号语义 | 保持/自动；不得破坏 OMML/OLE 或将标量误成向量。 | V0.4.2；关键词中的符号、数学字体和对象哈希测试。 | 公式对象保护进入安全层，领域字形留在类型层。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-L-005 | 原稿无编号时不自动生成；编号样式不作为基础层固定值，由类型/出版社/任务决定，并在标题编号完成后处理。 | monograph-base 保护；science-engineering-monograph/equation-heavy 可增量 | 用户/出版社；已批准领域层/模块限公式编号 | 保持/QA；编号变化进入允许清单。 | V0.4.1 保护、V0.4.2 领域编号；无号保持、批准编号和顺序测试。 | FMT-EQ-302 的固定（章.序）从 base 移出。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-M-007 | 图题或最后一条图注后接正文/列表时段后 18 pt；接标题、图片、表格、独立公式或分页时不增加。禁止插入真实空白段模拟间隔。 | monograph-base | 用户/出版社可覆盖段距；禁空段不可覆盖 | 自动；依据后续语义对象而非当前页猜测。 | V0.4.1；六类后续对象、分页和幂等测试。 | 替代旧插入真实空段及 FMT-CAP-301 段后 0。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "61294c93f4a652c0f3826785459c4dae1af652c356a9ec42b92af2e53791f4c9",

        "literal": "| V040-M-009 | 保留现有图号；只有批准后才能在标题编号完成后重建；装饰图片不编号。工程剖面、节点和详图标识由 engineering-drawing 分类。 | monograph-base 保护 + engineering-drawing 增量 | 用户/出版社；模块只限工程图标识 | 保持/QA；编号变化进入允许清单。 | V0.4.1 通用保护、V0.4.2 工程分类；装饰图和图纸标识测试。 | 保留 FMT-CAP-301 manual/preserve/no-auto-renumber；领域判断移入模块。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-S-003 | 代码块使用 Consolas/宋体 9 pt、左对齐、最小 15 pt、首行缩进 0、左缩进 2 字符、段前后 6 pt，不断字、不两端对齐；默认无语法高亮和边框。 | code-heavy | 用户/出版社；模块只限代码块 | 自动；不得修改真实空白和换行。 | V0.4.2；多行、中文注释、缩进和分页测试。 | 新建可选模块精确值。 |",

        "literal": "| V040-Z-001 | 建立完整字段台账，不执行 update-all；只更新获批字段，未知字段保持。 | 安全层 + monograph-base | 不可覆盖选择性边界 | 保持/自动/报告；字段代码与结果分别审计。 | V0.4.1；已知/未知字段和 story ranges 测试。 | 延续 V0.3.2 选择性回写，废弃全量更新。 |",

    {

      "lifecycle_source": {

        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-002 | STATE-Z-002+FIELD-ORDER-002 | P1/P3→P6/P7 | T41-Z-002 | MIG-Z-002：扩展单轮字段顺序 | 自动/阻塞；依赖顺序不可跳过 | V0.4.2 领域序列；V0.4.4 收敛 |",
        "literal_sha256": "612c23fad4e3a96e2e5e5a5ad448e9677778e3b8ffb754ae55d1e0759c62edb5",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"
      },
      "obligation_ids": [
        "V040-Z-002:registry",
        "V040-Z-002:asset",
        "V040-Z-002:preservation",
        "V040-Z-002:orchestration",
        "V040-Z-002:initial-formal-toc-update",
        "V040-Z-002:word-repagination-and-page-field-update",
        "V040-Z-002:toc-update-and-convergence-loop",
        "V040-Z-002:convergence-verification"
      ],
      "primary_version": "V0.4.1",
      "semantic_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 32900,
        "codepoint_start": 32557,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-002 | 固定顺序：语义目录 → 标题编号 → 附录/对象序列 → 定理/例题等序列 → 交叉引用关系及可选图表目录/索引来源准备 → 创建并首次更新正式 TOC → 目标 Word 重新分页与获批页面依赖字段选择性更新 → 再次更新 TOC 并进入分页/目录收敛循环 → 收敛后最终验收。 | monograph-base | 不可按模块或 Agent 自行改序；用户只能决定是否启用可选阶段 | 自动/阻塞；前一步未通过不得执行后一步，不能把首次 TOC 页码视为最终结果。 | V0.4.1 基础顺序、V0.4.2 领域序列、V0.4.4 Word 收敛；依赖顺序和首次 TOC 非终态测试。 | 扩展旧单轮字段顺序，补定理/例题序列和分页/目录收敛阶段。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "5c0682442db10d585f2b6313585b8395906c5d91828e13089fcab2db8599f3b0",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    },
    {
      "decision_id": "V040-Z-003",
      "lifecycle_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 33632,
        "codepoint_start": 33523,
        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-003 | STATE-Z-003 | P1/P3→P6 | T41-Z-003 | MIG-Z-003：升级章节不触发全书分页为硬门禁 | 自动/阻塞；仅表达批次约束 | V0.4.3 批次实现 |",
        "literal_sha256": "77b220697197c01378974983bd620e414f64c1055bdc48671d8bd0918574ebf8",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"

      "obligation_ids": [
        "V040-Z-003:registry",
        "V040-Z-003:asset",
        "V040-Z-003:preservation",
        "V040-Z-003:orchestration"
      ],
      "primary_version": "V0.4.1",
      "semantic_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 33087,
        "codepoint_start": 32901,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-003 | 每章只更新状态、清单和对象哈希，正式 TOC 只在全书阶段创建；每章不得重建 TOC、重新分页或更新全书页面依赖字段。 | monograph-base 长文运行约束 | 不可覆盖 | 自动/阻塞；章节批次不得触发全书分页。 | V0.4.1 规则表达、V0.4.3 运行实现；章节批次测试。 | 将 V0.3.0 的阶段策略设为硬门禁。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "f26ff39937fa116371578ec5c2290f575a457a22abc3d00ced6dffe7b94402f1",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    },
    {
      "decision_id": "V040-Z-004",
      "lifecycle_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 33756,
        "codepoint_start": 33633,
        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-004 | SAFE-Z-004+FIELD-WL-004 | P1/P3→P6/P7 | T41-Z-004 | MIG-Z-004：选择性回写增加收敛前禁写 | 自动/阻塞；允许变化审计 | V0.4.4 Word 复验 |",
        "literal_sha256": "3fb740bb688fbfd6e65d77d37d319d27cf075e9368611baf0b3a664223819a51",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"
      },
      "obligation_ids": [
        "V040-Z-004:registry",
        "V040-Z-004:asset",
        "V040-Z-004:preservation",
        "V040-Z-004:orchestration",
        "V040-Z-004:temporary-word-finalization",
        "V040-Z-004:whitelist-field-result-update",
        "V040-Z-004:controlled-result-writeback",
        "V040-Z-004:writeback-eligibility-verification"
      ],
      "primary_version": "V0.4.1",
      "semantic_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 33445,
        "codepoint_start": 33088,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-004 | 所有分页/目录收敛操作只在一次性目标 Microsoft Word 临时副本中执行；仅选择性更新白名单内的 TOC、PAGE、NUMPAGES、SECTIONPAGES、PAGEREF、获批交叉引用及其他获批页面依赖字段，不执行 update-all。只有收敛并通过差异审计的字段结果才可受控回写基准稿；临时副本存在未批准变化或结果未收敛时拒绝回写。 | 安全层 + monograph-base | 不可覆盖目标 Word 终验和选择性回写边界 | 自动/阻塞；字段代码、结果、对象关系和允许变化逐项审计。 | V0.4.1 字段白名单契约、V0.4.4 Word 收敛；未知字段保持、未批准变化拒绝和未收敛不回写测试。 | 继承 V0.3.2 选择性回写并增加收敛前禁止回写。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "2668ed55ac21d3126457c95a826a91559427dd0816969a236af127ed7efef1e8",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    },
    {
      "decision_id": "V040-Z-005",
      "lifecycle_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 33851,

        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-005 | REPORT-Z-005 | P1/P3→P6 | T41-Z-005 | MIG-Z-005：扩展综合报告为八类 QA | 报告/阻塞；缺类失败 | - |",
        "literal_sha256": "521fa107bfab56571c03435a8fe356af19f250c7bd230efb4114dad989938252",

        "literal": "| V040-Z-005 | QA 分开报告结构、内容、格式、字段、目标 Word、视觉、幂等性和恢复。 | 安全层 + monograph-base | 不可合并为模糊“通过” | 报告/阻塞。 | V0.4.1 报告契约；八类状态完整性测试。 | 扩展旧综合报告。 |",

        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"

    {
      "decision_id": "V040-Z-006",
      "lifecycle_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 33969,
        "codepoint_start": 33852,
        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-006 | STATE-Z-006 | P1/P3→P6 | T41-Z-006 | MIG-Z-006：收紧状态模型并禁未触达 | 自动/阻塞；V0.4.1 仅 schema 表达 | V0.4.3 全对象台账 |",

        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"

        "literal": "| V040-Z-006 | 全对象状态只能为 approved、formatted、unchanged、blocked_qa、failed；正式闭合时 approved=0，且其余四类合计等于全部对象。 | 安全层 + monograph-base 长文契约 | 不可覆盖 | 自动/阻塞；禁止未触达未报告。 | V0.4.1 schema 表达、V0.4.3 台账实现；对象守恒测试。 | 继承并收紧 V0.3.0 状态模型。 |",

    {

      "lifecycle_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 34099,
        "codepoint_start": 33970,
        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-007 | SAFE-Z-007+GATE-Z-007 | P1/P3→P6/P7 | T41-Z-007 | MIG-Z-007：禁止抽样/PDF/LibreOffice 冒充终验 | 阻塞/报告；声明受限 | V0.4.4 整书视觉 |",
        "literal_sha256": "9f3a68395b34bed7df2c31a938909c7dd100388ba8499e69bbe094daad6263a3",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"
      },
      "obligation_ids": [
        "V040-Z-007:registry",
        "V040-Z-007:asset",
        "V040-Z-007:preservation",
        "V040-Z-007:orchestration",
        "V040-Z-007:word-verification"
      ],
      "primary_version": "V0.4.1",
      "semantic_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 34069,
        "codepoint_start": 33801,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-007 | 所有页面进入渲染与自动异常检测，高风险页详细人工核查；目标 Microsoft Word 是最终分页、目录收敛和版面声明的必需条件。LibreOffice 或 PDF 只能提供补充视觉证据，不得替代目标 Word 的分页与收敛验收。未完成视觉 QA 时明确“不能宣称整书通过”。 | 安全层 | 不可覆盖 | 阻塞/报告；抽样只可作阶段证据。 | V0.4.1 门禁表达、V0.4.4 整书实现；逐页覆盖、后端差异和声明测试。 | 延续治理规则并禁止以 PDF/LibreOffice 替代 Word。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "e756eb1686a287005eb5245eae04d9757354968f567ec5036129512ca3c4c4a8",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    },
    {
      "decision_id": "V040-Z-008",
      "lifecycle_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 36505,
        "codepoint_start": 36411,
        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-008 | V0.4.3 | LEDGER-Z-008+RECOVERY-Z-008 | T43-Z-008 | V0.4.1 不实现检查点、resume 或幂等恢复 |",
        "literal_sha256": "ed6e7d0444a479af6f4178bbd5a511fbba5632fe655ab88936f56be19780dfe4",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"
      },
      "obligation_ids": [
        "V040-Z-008:future-primary"
      ],
      "primary_version": "V0.4.3",
      "semantic_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 34237,
        "codepoint_start": 34070,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-008 | 连续运行两次结果幂等；恢复使用原子检查点；不物理拆分/拼接 DOCX。 | 安全层 + monograph-base 长文契约 | 不可覆盖 | 阻塞；失败批次不得污染已完成批次。 | V0.4.3 恢复、V0.4.4 长文幂等；中断/恢复/二次运行测试。 | 继承 V0.3.0 并冻结不拆分策略。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "560a85050c3fd89e5adbc06097f6b138db8300c329de7d494b8b77326ac6b642",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    },
    {
      "decision_id": "V040-Z-009",
      "lifecycle_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 34202,
        "codepoint_start": 34100,
        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-009 | GOV-Z-009 | P1→P6/P7 | T41-Z-009 | MIG-Z-009：明确不迁移为公共 CLI | 保持/报告；仅内部接口 | 后续 CLI 独立方案 |",
        "literal_sha256": "6f000868a69a976521c27866c8bf9b57af48f2f3a6674b9d3fe1904fe7686264",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"
      },
      "obligation_ids": [
        "V040-Z-009:internal-interface",
        "V040-Z-009:public-wrapper-prohibition",
        "V040-Z-009:full-project-tests",
        "V040-Z-009:future-user-decision"
      ],
      "primary_version": "V0.4.1",
      "semantic_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 34378,
        "codepoint_start": 34238,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-009 | CLI 继续只作为内部执行和测试接口；任何对外封装须在全项目测试后经用户单独确认并另建方案。 | 治理边界 | 仅用户未来独立方案可改变 | 保持/报告。 | V0.4.1–V0.4.4 确认无公共 API 承诺。 | 不迁移为稳定公共 CLI。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "3813dd0ffb12e2859176ea5f0062a2ee46ceaf08353a72d627c5ccdba76927b4",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    },
    {
      "decision_id": "V040-Z-010",
      "lifecycle_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 34298,
        "codepoint_start": 34203,
        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-010 | SAFE-Z-010 | P1/P3→P6 | T41-Z-010 | MIG-Z-010：删除内容编辑授权例外 | 阻塞/报告；只允许受控结构变化 | - |",
        "literal_sha256": "fcf0a1de7db909bded885569dca5f4c79be01297323d685ddaa30655460f4284",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"
      },
      "obligation_ids": [
        "V040-Z-010:registry",
        "V040-Z-010:asset",
        "V040-Z-010:preservation",
        "V040-Z-010:orchestration"
      ],
      "primary_version": "V0.4.1",
      "semantic_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 34590,
        "codepoint_start": 34379,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-010 | 所有获批标题编号前缀删除、Word 派生字段结果、目录/页码/交叉引用等结构变化必须进入允许变化清单、对象哈希和字段审计；文字、标点、事实、数据、公式语义、题注文字、答案和参考文献内容始终不可修改。 | 安全层 | 不可覆盖 | 阻塞/报告；内容问题转 Skill 外工作流。 | V0.4.1；允许变化白名单与负面内容写入测试。 | 删除旧“另行授权内容编辑”例外，并统一结构变化审计。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "5c432bb3708268d46c5df6af4c73b3964fb0cdcbbe89c09c5e633cdbaa3305cf",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    },
    {
      "decision_id": "V040-Z-011",
      "lifecycle_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 34407,
        "codepoint_start": 34299,
        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-011 | CONV-Z-011 | P1/P3→P6/P7 | T41-Z-011 | MIG-Z-011：替代单轮最终化 | 自动/阻塞；快照维度全比较 | V0.4.4 Word 规模复验 |",
        "literal_sha256": "2453dcdc4993f5b619e0c54089d7c84eec9e6b5a12a3ef38aea796d148cd8999",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"
      },
      "obligation_ids": [
        "V040-Z-011:registry",
        "V040-Z-011:asset",
        "V040-Z-011:preservation",
        "V040-Z-011:orchestration",
        "V040-Z-011:formal-toc-update",
        "V040-Z-011:target-word-repagination",
        "V040-Z-011:approved-page-field-update",
        "V040-Z-011:convergence-snapshot-verification"
      ],
      "primary_version": "V0.4.1",
      "semantic_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 34915,
        "codepoint_start": 34591,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-011 | 一轮收敛定义为“更新正式 TOC → 在目标 Word 中重新分页 → 选择性更新获批页面依赖字段”。连续两轮必须逐项比较并完全一致：目录条目数量、顺序和文字；每个目录条目的页码；目录总页数；正文起始物理页和逻辑页码；各节页码起始值和编号格式；文档总页数；PAGE、NUMPAGES、SECTIONPAGES、PAGEREF 等获批页面依赖字段结果。 | 安全层 + monograph-base | 不可降低比较范围；字段白名单保持不变 | 自动/阻塞；任一比较项变化都视为未收敛。 | V0.4.1 收敛快照契约、V0.4.4 目标 Word 实现；全部比较维度及差异定位测试。 | 取代旧单轮字段最终化假设。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "c3210824990aa6f46a5be0307449aa5af1506756469cbcccaca39799638d2cb1",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    },
    {
      "decision_id": "V040-Z-012",
      "lifecycle_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 34519,
        "codepoint_start": 34408,
        "git_blob_oid": "4cb9119ccc91ec39876590a26ca0d7e8745c6d3b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-012 | CONV-Z-012 | P1/P3→P6/P7 | T41-Z-012 | MIG-Z-012：新增两轮稳定/三轮上限 | 自动/阻塞；不稳定零回写 | V0.4.4 Word 规模复验 |",
        "literal_sha256": "c928be800a38d2a31540df645c69d3a2202029b153db39e37426124d11cebbab",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.1-profile-foundation-and-grouped-execution.md"
      },
      "obligation_ids": [
        "V040-Z-012:registry",
        "V040-Z-012:asset",
        "V040-Z-012:preservation",
        "V040-Z-012:orchestration",
        "V040-Z-012:bounded-convergence-rounds",
        "V040-Z-012:bounded-convergence-verification"
      ],
      "primary_version": "V0.4.1",
      "semantic_source": {
        "codepoint_end": 35213,
        "codepoint_start": 34916,
        "git_blob_oid": "1d5b43979987744b57a7b6b686f2f3cbbffc3f0b",
        "literal": "| V040-Z-012 | 默认最多执行 3 轮收敛。第 1 轮由首次 TOC 后的重新分页和页面依赖字段更新形成；后续轮重复完整收敛操作。只有任意连续两轮的前述收敛快照完全一致才通过；第 3 轮后仍变化必须进入 blocked_qa，不得继续猜测、回写不稳定结果或宣称整书完成。 | 安全层 + monograph-base | 不可提高默认轮数规避阻塞；未来改变须单独方案和 QA | 自动/阻塞；保留每轮快照、差异和停止原因。 | V0.4.1 状态契约、V0.4.4 Word 循环；两轮稳定、第三轮稳定、三轮不稳定和不回写测试。 | 新增有界收敛门禁，防止无限循环和不稳定交付。 |",
        "literal_sha256": "ec80f6c2bbe2b9c4b1b1dcb8bb8616b414e6ce915c7db745ccd6540c86c11dfc",
        "path": "docs/plans/v0.4.0-profile-system-and-long-document-reliability.md"
      }
    }
  ],
